# Excluded artifacts

An exclusion applies to the named occurrence and all its descendants unless an explicit selected package/file in SOURCE_PACKAGES.json or RECOVERED_FILES.json overrides it. This rule covers every nested historical copy without reproducing its contents. `!/` denotes an archive boundary. No ZIP files are committed. Source hashes and the unchanged local ZIP retain the audit trail.

## Classification

- A: Current source: selected Part-A cores authenticated by the freeze plus `_lang`.
- B/F: Historical checkpoints, lineage, reference capsules and nested backup ZIPs: excluded from the live tree, except the authenticated Section 12 recovery below.
- C: Exact redundant copies: per-file rows identify the retained equivalent. Alternate Section 12 is superseded by the frozen lock. Required vendored imports are retained.
- D: Generated reference builds, execution output and bytecode caches: source/tests retained. Release evidence in `99_RELEASE` is retained as provenance.
- E: Historical dependency indexes, including every oversized RP42 index: descendants of excluded API/source capsules. These are not live Genesis package manifests or lockfiles. Live package-resolution source and frozen release locks remain.
- G: AppleDouble metadata (`__MACOSX/**`, `._*`), `.DS_Store`, and Python temporary caches are packaging/intermediate artifacts, not language source.
